============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

   195	function PhonePreview({
   196	  title,
   197	  metric,
   198	  variant,
   199	  href,
   200	  screenImageSrc,
   201	  screenImageAlt,
   202	}: {
   203	  href?: string;
   204	  title: string;
   205	  metric: string;
   206	  variant: "commerce" | "finance";
   207	  screenImageSrc?: string;
   208	  screenImageAlt?: string;
   209	}) {
   210	  const className = `phone-shell phone-shell-${variant}${
   211	    screenImageSrc ? " phone-shell-capture" : ""
   212	  }${href ? " phone-shell-link" : ""}`;
   213	  const content = screenImageSrc ? (
   214	    <Image
   215	      alt={screenImageAlt ?? `${title} app screen`}
   216	      className="phone-framed-image"
   217	      fill
   218	      sizes="245px"
   219	      src={screenImageSrc}
   220	    />
   221	  ) : (
   222	    <>
   223	      <div className="phone-speaker" />
   224	      <div className="phone-screen">
   225	        <div className="phone-status">
   226	          <span>{title}</span>
   227	          <span>5G</span>
   228	        </div>
   229	        <div className="phone-chart">
   230	          <div />
   231	          <div />
   232	          <div />
   233	          <div />
   234	        </div>
   235	        <div className="phone-cardline wide" />
   236	        <div className="phone-cardline" />
   237	        <div className="phone-grid">
   238	          <span />
   239	          <span />
   240	          <span />
   241	          <span />
   242	        </div>
   243	        <div className="phone-cta">{metric}</div>
   244	      </div>
   245	    </>
   246	  );
   247	
   248	  if (href) {
   249	    return (
   250	      <a
   251	        aria-label={`Open ${title} case study`}
   252	        className={className}
   253	        href={href}
   254	      >
   255	        {content}
   256	      </a>
   257	    );
   258	  }
   259	
   260	  return <div className={className}>{content}</div>;
   261	}

    74	function getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset() {
    75	  const isWideLandscape = window.matchMedia(
    76	    "(min-width: 1024px) and (orientation: landscape)",
    77	  ).matches;
    78	  const isPortrait = window.matchMedia("(orientation: portrait)").matches;
    79	
    80	  if (isWideLandscape) {
    81	    return Math.max(18, Math.min(44, window.innerHeight * 0.035));
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  if (isPortrait) {
    85	    return Math.max(14, Math.min(36, window.innerHeight * 0.03));
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  return Math.max(16, Math.min(40, window.innerHeight * 0.0325));
    89	}
    90	
    91	function scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(caseStudyId: string) {
    92	  const targetCard = document.getElementById(caseStudyId);
    93	
    94	  if (!targetCard) {
    95	    return;
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  const caseGrid = targetCard.closest<HTMLElement>(".case-grid");
    99	  const targetTop = targetCard.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   100	  const rowCards = caseGrid
   101	    ? Array.from(caseGrid.children).filter((child): child is HTMLElement => {
   102	        if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) {
   103	          return false;
   104	        }
   105	
   106	        const cardTop = child.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   107	
   108	        return Math.abs(cardTop - targetTop) <= 8;
   109	      })
   110	    : [targetCard];
   111	  const rowTop = Math.min(
   112	    ...rowCards.map((card) => card.getBoundingClientRect().top),
   113	  );
   114	
   115	  window.scrollTo({
   116	    behavior: "smooth",
   117	    top: window.scrollY + rowTop - getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset(),
   118	  });
   119	
   120	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", `#${caseStudyId}`);
   121	}

   171	function PhoneMark({
   172	  mobilePlatforms,
   173	}: {
   174	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   175	}) {
   176	  return (
   177	    <svg
   178	      aria-hidden

   183	      width="1.36125em"

   186	        className="phone-mini-frame"

   188	        rx="2.7"
   189	        width="19"

   307	function getPlatformListGridTemplate(columnCount: number) {
   308	  const columns = ["max-content", "1px"];
   309	
   310	  Array.from({ length: columnCount }).forEach((_, index) => {
   311	    columns.push("max-content");
   312	
   313	    if (index < columnCount - 1) {
   314	      columns.push("1px");
   315	    }
   316	  });
   317	
   318	  return columns.join(" ");
   319	}

   449	    >
   450	      <path
   451	        d="M12 6.5V3.4c0-.9.7-1.6 1.6-1.6h.3"
   452	        fill="none"
   453	        stroke="#000"
   454	        strokeLinecap="round"
   455	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   456	        strokeWidth="4"
   457	      />
   458	      <path
   459	        d="M17.8 21.1c-1.1.7-2.1.8-3 .3-1.7-.8-3.1-.8-4.8 0-1 .5-2 .4-3-.3C4.8 19.5 3.2 15.2 3.4 11.6c.2-3.2 2.4-5.4 5-5.4 1.4 0 2.4.7 3.2.7.7 0 1.8-.7 3.2-.7 2.2 0 4.1 1.5 4.8 3.7-1.6.7-2.4 1.9-2.4 3.5 0 1.8 1 3.1 2.6 3.7-.5 1.6-1.2 3-2 4Z"
   460	        fill="#f3fff7"
   461	        stroke="#000"
   462	        strokeLinecap="round"
   463	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   464	        strokeWidth="4"
   465	      />
   466	    </svg>
   467	  );
   468	}
   469	
   470	function PlatformLogoMark({ platform }: { platform: MobilePlatform }) {
   471	  return platform === "android" ? (
   472	    <AndroidPlatformMark />
   473	  ) : (
   474	    <ApplePlatformMark />
   475	  );
   476	}
   477	
   478	function FactorDeviceMark({ device }: { device: "phone" | "tablet" }) {
   479	  return device === "phone" ? (
   480	    <PhoneMark mobilePlatforms={[]} />
   481	  ) : (
   482	    <TabletMark showPlatformIcon={false} />
   483	  );
   484	}
   485	
   486	export function ProjectDeviceStack({
   487	  assetGap,
   488	  color,
   489	  hasTablet,
   490	  hasWeb,
   491	  mobilePlatforms,
   492	  responsiveAssetGap,
   493	  showPlatformLabels,
   494	  webIconScale,
   495	}: {
   496	  assetGap?: string;
   497	  color: string;
   498	  hasTablet?: boolean;
   499	  hasWeb?: boolean;
   500	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   501	  responsiveAssetGap?: ResponsiveAssetGap;
   502	  showPlatformLabels?: boolean;
   503	  webIconScale?: number;
   504	}) {
   505	  const stackRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
   506	  const [measuredAssetGap, setMeasuredAssetGap] = useState<string | null>(null);
   507	  const stackStyle: CSSProperties & {
   508	    "--project-device-stack-gap"?: string;
   509	  } = {
   510	    color,
   511	  };
   512	  const stackGap = measuredAssetGap ?? assetGap;
   513	
   514	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   515	    if (!responsiveAssetGap) {
   516	      setMeasuredAssetGap(null);
   517	      return;
   518	    }
   519	
   520	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   521	    const measurementTarget = stack?.parentElement ?? stack;
   522	
   523	    if (!measurementTarget) {
   524	      return;
   525	    }
   526	
   527	    let frame = 0;
   528	    const updateGap = () => {
   529	      if (frame) {
   530	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   531	      }
   532	
   533	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   534	        const laneWidth = measurementTarget.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   535	        const activeRange = Math.max(
   536	          1,
   537	          responsiveAssetGap.wideLanePx - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx,
   538	        );
   539	        const progress = Math.min(
   540	          1,
   541	          Math.max(
   542	            0,
   543	            (laneWidth - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx) / activeRange,
   544	          ),
   545	        );
   546	        const nextGap =
   547	          responsiveAssetGap.minPx +
   548	          (responsiveAssetGap.maxPx - responsiveAssetGap.minPx) * progress;
   549	        const nextGapValue = `${nextGap.toFixed(2)}px`;
   550	
   551	        setMeasuredAssetGap((currentGap) =>
   552	          currentGap === nextGapValue ? currentGap : nextGapValue,
   553	        );
   554	      });
   555	    };
   556	
   557	    updateGap();
   558	
   559	    const resizeObserver =
   560	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   561	        ? null
   562	        : new ResizeObserver(updateGap);
   563	
   564	    resizeObserver?.observe(measurementTarget);
   565	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   566	
   567	    return () => {
   568	      if (frame) {
   569	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   570	      }
   571	
   572	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   573	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   574	    };
   575	  }, [
   576	    responsiveAssetGap?.maxPx,
   577	    responsiveAssetGap?.minPx,
   578	    responsiveAssetGap?.tightLanePx,
   579	    responsiveAssetGap?.wideLanePx,
   580	  ]);
   581	
   582	  if (stackGap) {
   583	    stackStyle["--project-device-stack-gap"] = stackGap;
   584	  }
   585	
   586	  if (showPlatformLabels) {
   587	    const platforms: Array<MobilePlatform | "web"> = [];
   588	    const factorDevices: Array<"phone" | "tablet"> = [];
   589	
   590	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("android") || hasTablet) {
   591	      platforms.push("android");
   592	    }
   593	
   594	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("apple")) {
   595	      platforms.push("apple");
   596	    }
   597	
   598	    if (hasWeb) {
   599	      platforms.push("web");
   600	    }
   601	
   602	    if (mobilePlatforms.length > 0) {
   603	      factorDevices.push("phone");
   604	    }
   605	
   606	    if (hasTablet) {
   607	      factorDevices.push("tablet");
   608	    }
   609	
   610	    const platformLabels = platforms.map((platform) =>
   611	      platform === "web" ? "WEB" : platform === "android" ? "AND" : "IOS",
   612	    );
   613	    const factorDeviceLabels = factorDevices.map((device) =>
   614	      device === "phone" ? "PHONE" : "TABLET",
   615	    );
   616	    const labelColumnCount = Math.max(
   617	      platformLabels.length,
   618	      factorDeviceLabels.length,
   619	      1,
   620	    );
   621	    const dividerGridRow = factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? "1 / 3" : 1;
   622	    const platformListStyle = {
   623	      ...stackStyle,
   624	      gridTemplateColumns: getPlatformListGridTemplate(labelColumnCount),
   625	    };
   626	
   627	    return (
   628	      <span
   629	        className="carousel-button-project case-card-title-platform-list"
   630	        ref={stackRef}
   631	        style={platformListStyle}
   632	      >
   633	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-platform">
   634	          PLATFORM
   635	        </span>
   636	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   637	          gridColumn={2}
   638	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   639	          kind="grid"
   640	        />
   641	        <PlatformLabelSlots labels={platformLabels} row={1} />
   642	        <PlatformColumnDividers
   643	          columnCount={labelColumnCount}
   644	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   645	        />
   646	        {factorDevices.length > 0 ? (
   647	          <>
   648	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-factor">
   649	              DEVICE
   650	            </span>
   651	            <PlatformLabelSlots labels={factorDeviceLabels} row={2} />
   652	          </>
   653	        ) : null}
   654	      </span>
   655	    );
   656	  }
   657	
   658	  return (
   659	    <span className="carousel-button-project" ref={stackRef} style={stackStyle}>
   660	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform) => (
   661	        <PhoneMark key={platform} mobilePlatforms={[platform]} />
   662	      ))}
   663	      {hasTablet ? <TabletMark /> : null}
   664	      {hasWeb ? <WebMark scale={webIconScale} /> : null}
   665	    </span>
   666	  );
   667	}
   668	
   669	function CarouselArrowMark() {
   670	  return (
   671	    <img
   672	      alt=""
   673	      aria-hidden
   674	      className="carousel-button-arrow-mark"
   675	      draggable={false}
   676	      style={{
   677	        display: "block",
   678	        height: "100%",
   679	        verticalAlign: "top",
   680	        width: "100%",
   681	      }}
   682	      src="/images/carousel-arrow.svg"
   683	    />
   684	  );
   685	}
   686	
   687	export function ProjectCarouselButton() {
   688	  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
   689	  const projectButtonRef = useRef<HTMLAnchorElement>(null);
   690	  const activeProject = projectLinks[activeIndex];
   691	
   692	  const handleArrowClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
   693	    event.preventDefault();
   694	    scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(activeProject.caseStudyId);
   695	  };
   696	
   697	  useEffect(() => {
   698	    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
   699	      setActiveIndex((currentIndex) => (currentIndex + 1) % projectLinks.length);
   700	    }, 5000);
   701	
   702	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
   703	  }, []);
   704	
   705	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   706	    let frame = 0;
   707	
   708	    const syncHeroActionGeometry = () => {
   709	      if (frame) {
   710	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   711	      }
   712	
   713	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   714	        const heroCopy = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-copy");
   715	        const heroActions =
   716	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   717	        const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   718	        const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   719	        const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   720	        const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   721	        const profilePhoto =
   722	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   723	        const socialButton =
   724	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-social-button");
   725	        const visualFrameSelectors = [
   726	          ".profile-photo",
   727	          ".phone-shell-commerce",
   728	          ".phone-shell-finance",
   729	          ".tablet-shell",
   730	        ];
   731	
   732	        if (proofPanel && profilePhoto) {
   733	          const proofRect = proofPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
   734	          const profileRect = profilePhoto.getBoundingClientRect();
   735	          const proofWidth = profileRect.right - proofRect.left;
   736	
   737	          if (proofWidth > 0) {
   738	            [heroCopy, proofPanel, stackRow].forEach((element) => {
   739	              element?.style.setProperty(
   740	                "--hero-proof-landscape-width",
   741	                `${proofWidth}px`,
   742	              );
   743	            });
   744	          }
   745	        }
   746	
   747	        if (heroVisual && navBar && stackRow) {
   748	          const isTwoColumnHero = window.matchMedia(
   749	            "(min-width: 981px)",
   750	          ).matches;
   751	
   752	          if (!isTwoColumnHero) {
   753	            heroVisual.style.removeProperty("--hero-visual-center-offset");
   754	          } else {
   755	            const visualRects = visualFrameSelectors
   756	              .map((selector) =>
   757	                document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector),
   758	              )
   759	              .filter((element): element is HTMLElement => Boolean(element))
   760	              .map((element) => element.getBoundingClientRect());
   761	
   762	            if (visualRects.length > 0) {
   763	              const navRect = navBar.getBoundingClientRect();
   764	              const stackRect = stackRow.getBoundingClientRect();
   765	              const visualTop = Math.min(
   766	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.top),
   767	              );
   768	              const visualBottom = Math.max(
   769	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.bottom),
   770	              );
   771	              const currentOffset =
   772	                Number.parseFloat(
   773	                  window
   774	                    .getComputedStyle(heroVisual)
   775	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-visual-center-offset"),
   776	                ) || 0;
   777	              const visualCenterWithoutOffset =
   778	                (visualTop + visualBottom) / 2 - currentOffset;
   779	              const targetCenter = (navRect.bottom + stackRect.top) / 2;
   780	              const nextOffset = targetCenter - visualCenterWithoutOffset;
   781	
   782	              heroVisual.style.setProperty(
   783	                "--hero-visual-center-offset",
   784	                `${nextOffset}px`,
   785	              );
   786	            }
   787	          }
   788	        }
   789	
   790	        if (heroActions && socialButton) {
   791	          const heroActionsWidth = heroActions.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   792	          const socialButtonHeight =
   793	            socialButton.getBoundingClientRect().height;
   794	
   795	          if (heroCopy && heroActionsWidth > 0) {
   796	            heroCopy.style.setProperty(
   797	              "--hero-text-stack-width",
   798	              `${heroActionsWidth}px`,
   799	            );
   800	          }
   801	
   802	          if (socialButtonHeight > 0) {
   803	            const setPx = (name: string, value: number) => {
   804	              heroActions.style.setProperty(name, `${value}px`);
   805	            };
   806	
   807	            setPx("--hero-social-button-height", socialButtonHeight);
   808	            setPx("--hero-social-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.295833);
   809	            setPx("--hero-social-icon-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.672);
   810	            const baseSocialButtonHeight = 48;
   811	            const baseSocialGap = baseSocialButtonHeight * 0.045833 + 1;
   812	            const stretchedSocialGap =
   813	              Math.max(0, socialButtonHeight - baseSocialButtonHeight) *
   814	              0.091666;
   815	
   816	            setPx("--hero-social-gap", baseSocialGap + stretchedSocialGap);
   817	            setPx("--hero-social-pad-x", socialButtonHeight / 8);
   818	            setPx("--hero-view-label-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.275);
   819	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
   820	              "--hero-eyebrow-font-size",
   821	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.367}px`,
   822	            );
   823	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
   824	              "--hero-lede-font-size",
   825	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.5}px`,
   826	            );
   827	            setPx(
   828	              "--hero-carousel-base-font-size",
   829	              socialButtonHeight * 0.34375,
   830	            );
   831	            setPx(
   832	              "--hero-carousel-name-font-size",
   833	              socialButtonHeight * 0.352917,
   834	            );
   835	            setPx(
   836	              "--hero-carousel-category-font-size",
   837	              socialButtonHeight * 0.176458,
   838	            );
   839	            setPx(
   840	              "--hero-carousel-arrow-font-size",
   841	              socialButtonHeight * 0.458333,
   842	            );
   843	          }
   844	        }
   845	
   846	        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("hero-stack-sync"));
   847	
   848	        const viewLabel =
   849	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
   850	        const githubText =
   851	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
   852	        const githubIcon =
   853	          document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
   854	
   855	        if (viewLabel && githubText && githubIcon) {
   856	          const viewLabelRect = viewLabel.getBoundingClientRect();
   857	          const githubTextRect = githubText.getBoundingClientRect();
   858	          const githubIconRect = githubIcon.getBoundingClientRect();
   859	          const clusterLeft = Math.min(githubTextRect.left, githubIconRect.left);
   860	          const stackLeft = clusterLeft - viewLabelRect.left;
   861	
   862	          viewLabel.style.setProperty(
   863	            "--hero-view-stack-left",
   864	            `${stackLeft}px`,
   865	          );
   866	        }
   867	      });
   868	    };
   869	
   870	    syncHeroActionGeometry();
   871	    window.addEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
   872	
   873	    const targetIcon =
   874	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".dribbble-icon-mark") ??
   875	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".linkedin-icon-mark");
   876	    const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   877	    const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   878	    const heroActions = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   879	    const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   880	    const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   881	    const profilePhoto = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   882	    const targetButton = targetIcon?.closest(".hero-social-button");
   883	    const viewLabel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
   884	    const githubText = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
   885	    const githubIcon =
   886	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
   887	    const githubButton = githubText?.closest(".github-button");
   888	    const resizeObserver =
   889	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   890	        ? null
   891	        : new ResizeObserver(syncHeroActionGeometry);
   892	
   893	    if (resizeObserver) {
   894	      if (projectButtonRef.current) {
   895	        resizeObserver.observe(projectButtonRef.current);
   896	      }
   897	
   898	      if (heroActions) {
   899	        resizeObserver.observe(heroActions);
   900	      }
   901	
   902	      if (navBar) {
   903	        resizeObserver.observe(navBar);
   904	      }
   905	
   906	      if (heroVisual) {
   907	        resizeObserver.observe(heroVisual);
   908	      }
   909	
   910	      if (stackRow) {
   911	        resizeObserver.observe(stackRow);
   912	      }
   913	
   914	      if (proofPanel) {
   915	        resizeObserver.observe(proofPanel);
   916	      }
   917	
   918	      if (profilePhoto) {
   919	        resizeObserver.observe(profilePhoto);
   920	      }
   921	
   922	      if (targetIcon) {
   923	        resizeObserver.observe(targetIcon);
   924	      }
   925	
   926	      if (targetButton) {
   927	        resizeObserver.observe(targetButton);
   928	      }
   929	
   930	      if (viewLabel) {
   931	        resizeObserver.observe(viewLabel);
   932	      }
   933	
   934	      if (githubText) {
   935	        resizeObserver.observe(githubText);
   936	      }
   937	
   938	      if (githubIcon) {
   939	        resizeObserver.observe(githubIcon);
   940	      }
   941	
   942	      if (githubButton) {
   943	        resizeObserver.observe(githubButton);
   944	      }
   945	    }
   946	
   947	    if ("fonts" in document) {
   948	      document.fonts.ready.then(syncHeroActionGeometry).catch(() => {});
   949	    }
   950	
   951	    return () => {
   952	      if (frame) {
   953	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   954	      }
   955	
   956	      window.removeEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
   957	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   958	    };
   959	  }, []);
   960	
   961	  return (
   962	    <span className="carousel-button-group">
   963	      <a
   964	        aria-label={`Open ${activeProject.name} project`}
   965	        className="button button-primary carousel-button"
   966	        href={activeProject.href}
   967	        ref={projectButtonRef}
   968	      >
   969	        <span className="carousel-button-window" aria-live="polite">
   970	          <span className="carousel-button-label" key={activeProject.href}>
   971	            <span
   972	              className={`carousel-button-action carousel-button-action-${activeProject.tone}`}
   973	              style={{
   974	                ...strokedWhiteTextStyle,
   975	                alignItems: "center",
   976	                color: projectNameColors[activeProject.tone],
   977	                display: "inline-flex",
   978	                flexDirection: "column",
   979	                fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-name-font-size)",
   980	                gap: "2.2px",
   981	                justifyContent: "center",
   982	                lineHeight: 0.95,
   983	              }}
   984	            >
   985	              <span
   986	                className="carousel-button-action-name"
   987	                style={{ fontSize: activeProject.nameFontSize }}
   988	              >
   989	                {activeProject.name}
   990	              </span>
   991	              <span
   992	                className="carousel-button-action-category"
   993	                style={{
   994	                  WebkitTextStroke: 0,
   995	                  color: "#000",
   996	                  fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-category-font-size)",
   997	                  fontWeight: 900,
   998	                  letterSpacing: "0.06em",
   999	                  lineHeight: 1,
  1000	                  textAlign: "center",
  1001	                  textShadow: "none",
  1002	                }}
  1003	              >
  1004	                {activeProject.category}
  1005	              </span>
  1006	            </span>
  1007	            <ProjectDeviceStack
  1008	              color={projectNameColors[activeProject.tone]}
  1009	              hasTablet={activeProject.hasTablet}
  1010	              hasWeb={activeProject.hasWeb}
  1011	              mobilePlatforms={activeProject.mobilePlatforms}
  1012	            />
  1013	          </span>
  1014	        </span>
  1015	      </a>
  1016	      <a
  1017	        aria-label={`Scroll to ${activeProject.name} case study`}
  1018	        className="button button-primary carousel-arrow-button"
  1019	        href={`#${activeProject.caseStudyId}`}
  1020	        onClick={handleArrowClick}
  1021	      >
  1022	        <span aria-hidden className="carousel-button-arrow">
  1023	          <CarouselArrowMark />
  1024	        </span>
  1025	      </a>
  1026	    </span>
  1027	  );
  1028	}

    29	body {
    30	  min-height: 100vh;
    31	  margin: 0;
    32	  background:
    33	    linear-gradient(rgba(67, 255, 146, 0.035) 1px, transparent 1px),
    34	    linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(67, 255, 146, 0.035) 1px, transparent 1px),

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Prahl.dev Portfolio Site
     2	
     3	Next.js + Tailwind CSS portfolio start page for Prahl.dev.
     4	
     5	## What is included
     6	
     7	- `app/page.tsx` - the homepage/start page.
     8	- `app/globals.css` - the green/black visual system and responsive layout.
     9	- `public/images/profile-placeholder.png` - generated AI profile placeholder; replace this with your own square photo later using the same filename.
    10	- `public/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png` - the original generated mockup reference.
    11	- `public/favicon.svg` - simple green/black monogram favicon.
    12	
    13	## Local development
    14	
    15	```sh
    16	npm install
    17	npm run dev
    18	```
    19	
    20	Open `http://localhost:3000`.
    21	
    22	## Production build
    23	
    24	For Vercel, Netlify, or another Next.js host:
    25	
    26	```sh
    27	npm run build
    28	```
    29	
    30	For a simple static host:
    31	
    32	```sh
    33	npm run build:static
    34	```
    35	
    36	Then upload the generated `out/` folder.
    37	
    38	## Replace the profile photo
    39	
    40	Put your real square profile photo at:
    41	
    42	```txt
    43	public/images/profile-placeholder.png
    44	```
    45	
    46	Keep the filename the same and rebuild.


============================================================
FILE: DEPLOYMENT.md
============================================================

     1	# Deployment Notes
     2	
     3	## Best Option: Vercel
     4	
     5	1. Upload this `portfolio-site` folder to a GitHub repo.
     6	2. Import the repo in Vercel.
     7	3. Use the default Next.js settings.
     8	4. Build command: `npm run build`.
     9	
    10	## Netlify
    11	
    12	1. Upload this folder to a GitHub repo.
    13	2. Connect the repo in Netlify.
    14	3. Build command: `npm run build`.
    15	4. Publish directory: `.next`.
    16	5. Enable Netlify's Next.js runtime if prompted.
    17	
    18	## Simple Static Hosting
    19	
    20	Use this when the host only accepts HTML/CSS/JS files:
    21	
    22	```sh
    23	npm install
    24	npm run build:static
    25	```
    26	
    27	Upload the contents of:
    28	
    29	```txt
    30	out/
    31	```
    32	
    33	This mode does not need a running Node.js server after upload.
    34	
    35	## Contact Button
    36	
    37	The contact button currently uses:
    38	
    39	```txt
    40	mailto:hello@example.com
    41	```
    42	
    43	Replace `hello@example.com` in `app/page.tsx` with your real email address.

FILE: .github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml
============================================================

     1	name: Build static export for Hostinger
     2	
     3	on:
     4	  push:
     5	    branches: [main]
     6	  workflow_dispatch:
     7	
     8	permissions:
     9	  contents: write
    10	
    11	concurrency:
    12	  group: hostinger-static-export
    13	  cancel-in-progress: true
    14	
    15	jobs:
    16	  build-and-publish:
    17	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    18	    steps:
    19	      - name: Check out source
    20	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    21	
    22	      - name: Set up Node
    23	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
    24	        with:
    25	          node-version: 22
    26	          cache: npm
    27	
    28	      - name: Install dependencies
    29	        run: npm ci
    30	
    31	      - name: Build static export
    32	        run: npm run build:static
    33	
    34	      - name: Publish static export branch
    35	        env:
    36	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    37	        run: |
    38	          cd out
    39	          git init -b hostinger-deploy
    40	          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
    41	          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
    42	          git add -A
    43	          git commit -m "Deploy static export from ${GITHUB_SHA}"
    44	          git remote add origin "https://x-access-token:${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git"
    45	          git push --force origin hostinger-deploy

FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/portfolio_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Portfolio project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Portfolio Next.js PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you do not understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	10. For this Portfolio repo/website project, always ask me for the commit message before committing or pushing.
    28	
    29	
    30	============================================================
    31	PORTFOLIO PROJECT NOTES
    32	============================================================
    33	
    34	- Main source branch: main
    35	- Deployment source: GitHub Actions builds main into the hostinger-deploy branch
    36	- Hostinger root directory: public_html
    37	- Main stack represented by the website: React / React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, Kotlin
    38	- Public website assets live under public/
    39	- Run this script before starting a Portfolio session to refresh the project snapshot.
    40	
    41	
    42	============================================================
    43	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    44	============================================================
    45	
    46	This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    47	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    48	
    49	The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
    50	- .git
    51	- node_modules
    52	- .next
    53	- out
    54	- dist
    55	- build
    56	- .cache
    57	- .turbo
    58	- .npm
    59	- .pnpm-store
    60	- .yarn
    61	- .DS_Store
    62	- next-env.d.ts
    63	- *.tsbuildinfo
    64	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    65	- *.zip
    66	- .env and .env*.local
    67	
    68	Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    69	
    70	
    71	============================================================
    72	PROJECT FILE TREE
    73	============================================================
    74	
    75	EOF
    76	}
    77	
    78	print_file() {
    79	  local file="$1"
    80	
    81	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    82	    {
    83	      echo ""
    84	      echo ""
    85	      echo "============================================================"
    86	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    87	      echo "============================================================"
    88	      echo ""
    89	      nl -ba "$file"
    90	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    91	  fi
    92	}
    93	
    94	print_inventory_header() {
    95	  local title="$1"
    96	
    97	  {
    98	    echo ""
    99	    echo ""
   100	    echo "============================================================"
   101	    echo "$title"
   102	    echo "============================================================"
   103	    echo ""
   104	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   105	}
   106	
   107	print_file_inventory_line() {
   108	  local file="$1"
   109	  local size
   110	  local modified
   111	
   112	  size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   113	  modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   114	  printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   115	}
   116	
   117	print_dir_inventory() {
   118	  local title="$1"
   119	  local dir="$2"
   120	
   121	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   122	
   123	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
   124	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   125	    return
   126	  fi
   127	
   128	  find "$dir" -type f \
   129	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   130	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   131	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   132	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   133	  done
   134	}
   135	
   136	print_matching_inventory() {
   137	  local title="$1"
   138	  shift
   139	
   140	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   141	
   142	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" "$@" -type f \
   143	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git/*" \
   144	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules/*" \
   145	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next/*" \
   146	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out/*" \
   147	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   148	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   149	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   150	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   151	  done
   152	}
   153	
   154	append_git_context() {
   155	  {
   156	    echo ""
   157	    echo ""
   158	    echo "============================================================"
   159	    echo "GIT CONTEXT"
   160	    echo "============================================================"
   161	    echo ""
   162	    echo "Current branch:"
   163	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true
   164	    echo ""
   165	    echo "Status:"
   166	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" status --short --branch 2>/dev/null || true
   167	    echo ""
   168	    echo "Recent commits:"
   169	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" log --oneline --decorate -10 2>/dev/null || true
   170	    echo ""
   171	    echo "Remotes:"
   172	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" remote -v 2>/dev/null || true
   173	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   174	}
   175	
   176	write_snapshot() {
   177	  write_header
   178	
   179	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   180	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   181	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules" -prune -o \
   182	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next" -prune -o \
   183	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out" -prune -o \
   184	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/dist" -prune -o \
   185	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   186	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   187	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.turbo" -prune -o \
   188	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.npm" -prune -o \
   189	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.pnpm-store" -prune -o \
   190	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.yarn" -prune -o \
   191	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   192	    -name "next-env.d.ts" -prune -o \
   193	    -name "*.tsbuildinfo" -prune -o \
   194	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   195	    -name "*.zip" -prune -o \
   196	    -name ".env" -prune -o \
   197	    -name ".env*.local" -prune -o \
   198	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   199	
   200	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   201	
   202	
   203	============================================================
   204	FILE CONTENTS - APP SOURCE
   205	============================================================
   206	
   207	EOF
   208	
   209	  for file in \
   210	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/layout.tsx" \
   211	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/page.tsx" \
   212	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/ProjectCarouselButton.tsx" \
   213	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/globals.css"
   214	  do
   215	    print_file "$file"
   216	  done
   217	
   218	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   219	
   220	
   221	============================================================
   222	FILE CONTENTS - ROOT / TOOLING / DEPLOYMENT
   223	============================================================
   224	
   225	EOF
   226	
   227	  for file in \
   228	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   229	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   230	    "$PROJECT_DIR/DEPLOYMENT.md" \
   231	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package.json" \
   232	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package-lock.json" \
   233	    "$PROJECT_DIR/next.config.ts" \
   234	    "$PROJECT_DIR/postcss.config.mjs" \
   235	    "$PROJECT_DIR/tsconfig.json" \
   236	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml" \
   237	    "$PROJECT_DIR/public/favicon.svg" \
   238	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   239	  do
   240	    print_file "$file"
   241	  done
   242	
   243	  append_git_context
   244	
   245	  print_dir_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY - PUBLIC IMAGES" "$PROJECT_DIR/public/images"
   246	  print_matching_inventory "PDF WORKLOG INVENTORY" \( -name "portfolio_worklog_*.pdf" \)
   247	  print_matching_inventory "DEPLOYMENT ARCHIVE INVENTORY" \( -name "*.zip" \)
   248	
   249	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   250	
   251	
   252	============================================================
   253	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   254	============================================================
   255	
   256	The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
   257	- .git
   258	- node_modules
   259	- .next
   260	- out
   261	- dist
   262	- build
   263	- .cache
   264	- .turbo
   265	- .npm
   266	- .pnpm-store
   267	- .yarn
   268	- .DS_Store
   269	- next-env.d.ts
   270	- *.tsbuildinfo
   271	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   272	- *.zip
   273	- .env and .env*.local
   274	
   275	The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
   276	
   277	EOF
   278	}
   279	
   280	write_snapshot
   281	
   282	echo "Done."
   283	echo ""
   284	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   285	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   286	echo ""
   287	echo "Open it with:"
   288	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""
